### [85] [Real-time SQL Plan Management in Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27758)
*Sunil Chakkappen,Mohamed Ziauddin,Hong Su,Shreya Kunjibettu,Nigel Bayliss*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Oracle 26ai中提出的Real-Time SPM，这是对传统SQL Plan Management（SPM）的扩展，通过在前台用户查询执行过程中对新执行计划进行即时验证，解决了传统后台验证机制的延迟问题，实现了快速采用优质计划和及时检测回归的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库升级、DML变更、新索引等因素会导致SQL执行计划变化，引发性能回归。虽然SPM通过冻结计划防止回归，但传统实现依赖后台性能验证过程，存在回归检测和恢复延迟的问题。在自主云数据库系统中，多个自动操作可能导致计划变更，且后台资源受限，无法及时跟上。

Method: Real-Time SPM在用户查询执行期间（前台）进行新执行计划的验证，利用运行时会话上下文立即验证计划变化，从而快速采用更优计划，同时及时检测和阻止回归。论文还解决了可靠比较先前计划性能的技术挑战。

Result: Real-Time SPM实现了对计划变更的即时验证，克服了传统后台计划演化的局限性，能够在用户查询执行过程中快速采用优越计划并防止回归。

Conclusion: Real-Time SPM是传统后台计划演化机制的有效替代方案，尤其适合自主云数据库场景，在保证计划稳定性的同时实现了及时的性能优化和回归防护。

Abstract: Consistent query performance is essential for mission critical database applications, yet SQL execution plans can change due to factors such as database upgrades, DML changes, new indexes, etc. While plan stability mechanisms such as stored outlines prevent regressions by freezing execution plans, they also inhibit performance improvements by disallowing plan evolution. We introduced SQL Plan Management (SPM) in Oracle 11g to address this trade-off by maintaining a set of accepted execution plans and allowing plan evolution only when new plans demonstrably outperform existing baselines. However, prior implementations of SPM primarily rely on background performance verification processes, delaying regression detection and recovery. This issue is amplified in autonomous cloud database systems, where several automatic actions that could cause plan change driven regressions are performed with limited customer control. Timely detection and remediation is paramount, but the constrained background resources on cloud may not keep pace. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Real-Time SPM in Oracle 26ai, a novel extension of SPM that performs foreground verification of new execution plans during user query execution. Real-Time SPM leverages runtime session context to immediately validate plan changes, enabling rapid adoption of superior plans while promptly detecting and preventing regressions. This paper presents the architecture and design of Real-Time SPM - including technical challenges like reliably comparing performance of previous plans - and contrasts it with traditional background plan evolution.

</details>


### [86] [ANCHOR: A Vision for Secure Persistent Key-Value Stores in Disaggregated Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27819)
*Viraj Thakkar,Dongha Kim,Hokeun Kim,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出ANCHOR，一种面向分解式持久化键值存储（PKVS）的端到端完整性与新鲜度保障架构愿景，通过持久化路径的加密认证与易失路径的可信策略两层设计来应对分解式架构中扩展的信任边界问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化键值存储正越来越多地部署在计算、内存和存储分离的分解式环境中，这重新划定了信任边界，数据从单一机器扩展到跨多主机传输、缓存和重写，增加了面对网络攻击者和基础设施内部对手的攻击面。现有方案缺乏对这种新型分解式架构下的端到端数据完整性与新鲜度保障。

Method: ANCHOR采用两部分语义感知架构：1) 持久化路径——对PKVS持久化文件进行加密和认证，并通过清单版本化（manifest versioning）防止回滚攻击；2) 易失路径——将缓存、索引和过滤器视为不可信提示，除非附带可验证的溯源信息，并由TEE驻留策略强制执行。此外，提出关键不变量，并讨论enclave友好的批处理和异步I/O以摊销验证开销。

Result: 论文提出了一系列关键不变量，并展示了通过enclave友好的批处理与异步I/O机制来分摊验证成本的方法，在不牺牲分解式架构性能和弹性优势的前提下实现完整的保障目标。

Conclusion: ANCHOR为分解式PKVS提供了一种端到端完整性保障的愿景，通过持久化与易失路径的协同设计，在分解式架构下重新建立可信的数据保障机制。

Abstract: Persistent key-value stores (PKVS) are increasingly deployed in disaggregated settings that split compute, memory, and storage across separate server pools. This shift redraws the trust boundary: data that would remain within a single machine is now transported, cached, and rewritten across multiple hosts, expanding exposure to both network attackers and intra-infrastructure adversaries.
  This paper presents ANCHOR, a vision for end-to-end integrity and freshness in disaggregated PKVS. ANCHOR proposes a two-part semantics-aware architecture: 1) Persistence path: ANCHOR outlines encrypting and authenticating PKVS persistent files and preventing rollback with manifest versioning. 2) Volatile path: ANCHOR treats caches, indexes, and filters as untrusted hints unless accompanied by verifiable provenance, enforced by a TEE-resident policy. Finally, we outline key invariants and discuss enclave-friendly batching and asynchronous I/O to amortize verification without undermining disaggregation's performance and elasticity benefits.

</details>


### [87] [DBRepro: Automated Database Synthesis via a Hybrid Constraint-Solving Approach for Reproducing Slow Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27822)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shuang Liu,Dengfeng Xu,Wei Lu,Jianquan Leng,Sheng Du,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DBRepro是一个自动化端到端框架，用于从非侵入式元数据合成代理数据库，以离线复现慢查询的物理执行计划，解决了数据库管理系统性能瓶颈诊断中无法在线诊断和复制生产数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢查询导致数据库性能瓶颈，在线诊断会加剧资源争用，而数据隐私法规禁止将生产数据复制到测试环境。现有的数据驱动和工作负载感知方法无法同时满足保持全局统计分布和强制执行精确局部基数的要求。

Method: 将数据库生成形式化为约束分布合成问题：从轻量级列统计初始化全局分布，从目标查询提取执行约束，然后逐步调整分布以在保持全局分布的同时满足这些约束。

Result: 在TPC-H和SSB上，相比数据驱动基线将基数误差降低最多20.3%，且保持相同的计划一致性；相比工作负载感知基线，复现一致执行计划多15%，延迟比例误差降低21.5%。在KingbaseES管理的近1TB真实数据集上也以高保真度复现了复杂慢查询的执行性能。

Conclusion: DBRepro通过约束分布合成方法，成功同时满足了全局分布保持和局部基数精确两个要求，为数据库慢查询的离线复现和诊断提供了高保真解决方案。

Abstract: Slow queries frequently cause severe performance bottlenecks in database management systems. Diagnosing their root causes online risks exacerbating resource contention, while data privacy regulations often prohibit copying production data to test environments. Synthesizing a proxy database from non-intrusive metadata that induces the query optimizer to generate the same physical execution plans is therefore critical for offline diagnosis. High-fidelity reproduction requires preserving global statistical distributions while enforcing exact local cardinalities. Existing data-driven and workload-aware approaches cannot satisfy both requirements simultaneously.
  We present DBRepro, an automated end-to-end framework that formulates database generation as a constrained distribution synthesis problem. DBRepro initializes a global distribution from lightweight column statistics, extracts execution constraints from target queries, and progressively adjusts the distribution to satisfy these constraints while preserving the global distribution. Experiments on TPC-H and SSB show that DBRepro reduces cardinality error by up to 20.3% over a data-driven baseline while maintaining identical plan consistency. Compared with a workload-aware baseline, it reproduces 15% more consistent execution plans and reduces latency proportion error by 21.5%. We further validate DBRepro on a nearly 1 TB real-world dataset managed by KingbaseES, where it reproduces the execution performance of complex slow queries with high fidelity.

</details>


### [88] [VeriTS: Verifiable Model-Enhanced Time-Series Queries on Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28318)
*Zhongming Yao,Jun Pang,Chenxu Wang,Qian Ma,Peiyuan Guan,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriTS，这是首个面向区块链系统的可验证时序查询框架，支持高效的范围查询和聚合查询而无需改变区块链存储结构，通过认证聚合区间树实现索引与认证数据结构合一，在窗口聚合上较逐记录证明提升两个数量级的验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链数据本质上是时序的（每笔交易带时间戳且链上强加全序），形成按源划分的时间序列流。然而现有系统仅支持区块和交易的基础查找，无法回答时间范围检索和窗口聚合等时序查询。将查询卸载到链下虽然恢复了表达力，但链下查询层不可信，因此结果必须可验证。

Method: VeriTS维护一个链下查询层，每个数据流通过认证聚合区间树表示。该树同时充当查询索引和认证数据结构，窗口聚合通过折叠对数数量级的节点聚合来回答。完整性通过最小覆盖集验证，可靠性通过聚合折叠保证。此外扩展到模型段上的近似路径，在有限误差下重新定义完整性与可靠性。矿工验证模型的残差而非重放其计算，使对抗性编码器只能夸大证明大小和答案宽度但无法破坏正确性。

Result: 实验表明，在窗口聚合上VeriTS的验证效率比逐记录证明提升两个数量级以上；范围查询证明缩小最多14.5倍。

Conclusion: VeriTS是首个支持区块链可验证时序查询的框架，在不改变底层存储结构的前提下实现高效且可验证的时间范围查询和窗口聚合，兼顾完整性与可靠性保证。

Abstract: Blockchain data is temporal. Every transaction carries a timestamp and the chain imposes a total order, so on-chain data forms per-source time-series streams. However, existing systems support only basic lookups on blocks and transactions, and cannot answer time-series queries such as time-range retrieval and windowed aggregation. Offloading queries off-chain restores expressiveness, but the off-chain query layer is untrusted, so results must be verifiable. To this end, we propose VeriTS, the first verifiable time-series query framework for blockchain systems. It supports efficient range and aggregation queries without altering blockchain storage structures. VeriTS maintains an off-chain query layer that represents each stream through an authenticated aggregate interval tree. The tree serves as the query index and as the authenticated data structure at once, so a windowed aggregate is answered by folding a logarithmic number of node aggregates. VeriTS verifies completeness through a minimum covering set and soundness through aggregate folding. It extends both guarantees to an approximate path over model segments, redefining completeness and soundness under bounded error. Miners validate a model's residual rather than replay its computation, so even an adversarial encoder can inflate proof size and answer width but never correctness. Experiments offer evidence that on windowed aggregation, VeriTS improves verification efficiency by more than two orders of magnitude over per-record proofs. Range-query proofs shrink by up to 14.5x.

</details>


### [89] [No Silver Bullet: Boosting GaussDB Performance on the 30TB TPC-H Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28352)
*Tim Zeyl,Jason Lam,Shu Lin,Reza Pournaghi,Qi Cheng,Calvin Wong,Kaixiang Du,Yuliang He,Yang Sun,Weicheng Wang,Paul Lee,Chen Ruo,Yang Xinyi,Li Qunan,Wang Junjie,Hu Dongxing,Chong Chen,Per-Ake Larson*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了华为GaussDB数据库系统为提升大规模复杂分析负载性能所做的一系列改进，包括采用流水线执行模型、更快的跨节点数据洗牌、统一总线和统一远程内存访问，以及扩展基于代价的Bloom filter放置并实现多种流式策略。改进后，GaussDB在30TB规模下TPC-H工作负载性能超过此前最佳公开结果40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaussDB作为华为面向大规模部署和严苛负载的分布式共享无状态数据库系统，需要应对大规模复杂分析工作负载的挑战，因此需要通过系统性优化来突破其性能瓶颈。

Method: 采用流水线执行模型提升执行效率；设计更快且可扩展的跨节点数据洗牌机制；利用统一总线和统一远程内存访问来加速数据交互；扩展基于代价的Bloom filter放置支持，并实现多种Bloom filter流式策略以支持跨节点使用。

Result: 经过上述改进后，GaussDB在30TB规模的TPC-H工作负载上性能超过此前最佳公开结果40%。

Conclusion: 通过执行模型、数据洗牌、内存访问和Bloom filter等多方面的协同优化，GaussDB在大型分析工作负载上获得了业界领先的性能表现。

Abstract: GaussDB is Huawei's premier database system, designed for large-scale deployments and the most demanding workloads. It is a distributed shared-nothing system, capable of handling all types of workloads. This paper outlines a series of modifications to GaussDB aimed at improving its performance on large-scale and complex analytical workloads. After these changes, its performance on the TPC-H workload exceeded the best published result by 40% at 30 TB.
  The key enhancements to achieve this elite performance include adopting a pipeline execution model, a faster and more scalable inter-node data shuffle, exploiting a unified bus and unified remote memory access. We also expanded the support of cost-based Bloom filter placement and implemented several Bloom filter streaming strategies, enabling their use across nodes.

</details>

### [90] [A Mixed-Behavior Vote Model for Multimedia Subjective Quality Votes, Means, and Variances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27724)
*Jaden Pieper,Stephen D. Voran*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“单峰方差区域”（UVR）的缩减允许方差区域，用于更好地描述多媒体主观评分行为；同时指出实践中常见的抛物线方差模型常违反允许区域，并提出了符合允许区域的替代模型，还提出了一种参数化随机过程来模拟投票行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明主观测试投票方差与均值（MOS）之间存在关系，但已有的数学允许方差区域对真实多媒体主观评分行为的描述不够准确，且常用的抛物线方差模型在实践中常违反允许区域，需要更符合实际行为的建模方法。

Method: 提出单峰方差区域（UVR）作为缩减的允许方差区域；提出尊重允许区域的替代方差模型；设计一个参数化随机过程，通过混合投票过程在任意目标MOS下生成UVR范围内的真实方差范围。

Result: 在16个数据集（涵盖语音、图像和视频主观质量实验）上进行了验证，该投票模型能够从主观实验中建模投票方差，并提供关于投票行为的可解释性见解。

Conclusion: 所提出的UVR和参数化投票模型能够更真实地描述多媒体主观投票行为，为理解实验中观察到的投票行为提供了额外且可解释的视角。

Abstract: The relationship between subjective test vote variance and vote mean (or MOS) is well-studied, and the mathematically admissible vote variance region has been previously defined. We propose a reduced admissible variance region called the Unimodal Variance Region (UVR) that better describes real subjective rating behavior of multimedia. Further, subjective vote variance is often modeled as parabolic. We explain that, in practice, the parabolic model often violates the admissible region in the variance vs. MOS plane and we propose alternatives that respect the admissible region. We also present a parametrized random process to model votes that mixes voting processes and produces a realistic range of vote variances within the UVR at any desired MOS. This process was inspired by and comports with voting behavior that is observed in many subjective tests. By modeling vote variance from a subjective experiment, this vote model offers additional interpretable insights into voting behavior observed in a given experiment. We present example results from 16 datasets spanning speech, image, and video subjective quality experiments.

</details>


### [91] [Agentic Artifact Creation: Systems, Evaluation, Principles, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28122)
*Tianfu Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Xilin Xia,Lixin Liu,Mengkang Hu,Hongzhang Liu,Xi Chen,Ziyan Liu,Xiankun Lin,Weijia Zhang,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本综述系统研究了"智能体工件创作"（agentic artifact creation）领域，通过分析230个系统与29个基准（截至2026年8月20日共259篇文献），对六类工件家族进行跨类别比较，并提出关于解耦、评估偏差以及可控性与问责制方面的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能低成本产出草稿和组件，但其实际影响取决于这些片段能否成为完整、可交付的成果。该领域缺乏对"智能体工件创作"的系统性审视，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者采用系统性综述方法，检索并筛选了2026年8月20日前可获取的259项工作（230个系统+29个基准），按六种工件家族进行对比，并将应用场景与评估实践作为独立维度进行分析。

Result: 研究发现：构建挑战不仅取决于模态，还取决于决策耦合紧密度与失败可发现性；分解可降低局部复杂度但会增加协调与重组成本；共享生成器偏好或盲区的学习型评判者可能无法提供独立证据。

Conclusion: 作者提出三项原则：保持承诺与责任明确、将反馈转化为针对性修复、变更后重新验证受影响状态；并识别出在工件、创作者意图和构建系统演化中维持一致且可问责控制的机会。

Abstract: Generative models can turn natural-language prompts into images, text, code, and other content, lowering the cost of producing drafts and components. Their practical impact increasingly depends on whether those pieces can become complete, dependable deliverables. This survey examines agentic artifact creation, which we define as stateful construction in which an AI system materially constructs or revises a deliverable and intermediate observations redirect later work. Functionally, the process links an operational representation of the artifact, a construction policy, and runtime verification whose feedback can redirect later actions. We reviewed 259 works available through August 20, 2026: 230 systems meeting this definition and 29 benchmarks of agentic artifact construction. We compare six artifact families, then analyze application settings and evaluation practice as separate dimensions. Across families, construction challenges reflect not only modality but also how tightly decisions are coupled and whether failures become visible while they remain repairable. Decomposition can reduce local complexity while increasing coordination and reassembly costs. Learned judges may add little independent evidence when they share the generator's preferences or blind spots. We formulate principles for keeping commitments and responsibility explicit, turning feedback into targeted repair, and revalidating affected state after change. We also identify opportunities for sustaining coherent, accountable control as artifacts, creator intent, and construction systems evolve. A curated paper list is available at https://github.com/GeminiLight/awesome-agentic-artifact-creation.

</details>


### [92] [MuSP-Bench: Advanced Multimodal Benchmarking of Music Understanding across Score and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28212)
*Milan Liessens Dujardin,Song-Ze Yu,Kevin Miao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了MuSP-Bench，一个包含490个由人类编写的问题的基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型对乐谱和演奏两种音乐模态的理解能力。实验发现前沿模型在这两类任务上表现均不佳，且对演奏音频的理解比对乐谱的理解更加困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐家在交流音乐时同时依赖乐谱（编码音乐意图）和演奏（以声音实现意图），然而现有的多模态大语言模型能否真正理解这两种模态尚不明确，因此需要系统性的基准来评估模型在这方面的能力。

Method: 构建了人类撰写的490道问题组成的MuSP-Bench基准，涵盖古典钢琴和管弦乐作品，包含基于乐谱的推理、基于演奏的推理、诠释性推理和长程推理四类任务；在多种输入条件下评估了前沿多模态大语言模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，前沿多模态大语言模型在理解乐谱方面存在显著困难，而在推理演奏音频时面临的挑战更大，模型整体表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型对音乐的理解能力仍非常有限，尤其在演奏音频推理方面更为薄弱，MuSP-Bench可作为未来音乐理解模型评估与发展的基准。

Abstract: Musicians commonly communicate music through scores and performances. Scores encode musical intent, while performances realize it in sound. To investigate whether models can meaningfully engage with both modalities, we introduce MuSP-Bench, a human-authored benchmark of 490 questions targeting understanding across Musical Scores and Performances. The benchmark distinguishes itself by spanning score-based, performance-based, interpretive, and long-horizon reasoning across classical piano and orchestral works. We evaluate frontier multimodal large language models under multiple input conditions. Our results show that these models struggle substantially to understand scores, while facing even greater challenges when reasoning about performance audio. The benchmark is available at https://musp.vaclis.net/.

</details>

### [93] [LitCurate: A Configuration-Driven AI-Assisted Framework for Scientific Database Construction with an Application to Lower-Mantle Equation-of-State Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27629)
*Abin Shakya,Wilson Samuels,Dominica Wilson,Gioia A. Marchi,Israa Draz,Chenxing Luo,Renata M. Wentzcovitch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LitCurate，一个基于大语言模型的开源框架，用于从科学文献中构建结构化数据库。该框架采用可审计的分阶段策展工作流，整合了文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取，并在构建下地幔矿物相状态方程数据库时进行了验证，包含来自205篇论文的1,334条记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献中积累了数十年的实验和计算结果，但这些信息大多被锁定在出版物中，难以用于大规模分析或科学软件。从文献中构建结构化数据库面临两大挑战：从大量论文中发现相关研究，以及在足够的科学语境下提取报告的量值。

Method: LitCurate采用大语言模型驱动的可审计分阶段策展工作流，集成文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取四个阶段，保留中间结果和来源追踪，允许研究者检查和修订各个阶段，而非将自动策展视为黑箱过程。

Result: 应用LitCurate构建了包含205篇论文、1,334条记录的下地幔及相关高压矿物相状态方程数据库，每条记录关联矿物相、成分、方程形式、方法和参数约束，并标注值的来源（原始报告或引用报告），数据可通过可搜索的Web应用访问。

Conclusion: LitCurate提供了一种可复用的方法，将积累的文献转化为可追踪、机器可读的数据资源，连接科学文献与科学分析和计算建模，为大规模文献数据构建提供了开放可审计的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing scientific literature contains decades of experimental and computational results that could support data-driven and physics-based modeling, yet much of this infor- mation remains locked in publications and is not readily usable for large-scale analysis or sci- entific software. Building structured databases from the literature is particularly challenging whenrelevantstudiesmustfirstbediscoveredamonglargecollectionsofpapersandreported quantities must be extracted with enough scientific context to remain usable. We present LitCurate, an open-source framework for building scientific databases from the literature using large language models within an auditable, stage-wise curation workflow. LitCurate integratesliteraturediscovery, relevancescreening, full-textprocessing, andstructuredinfor- mation extraction while retaining intermediate results and provenance, allowing researchers to inspect and revise individual stages rather than treating automated curation as a black- box process. We apply LitCurate to construct an equation-of-state database of lower-mantle and lower-mantle-relevant high-pressure mineral phases from experimental and theoretical studies, comprising 1,334 entries from 205 papers. The resulting dataset links reported equation-of-state parameters to mineral phases, compositions, equation formulations, meth- ods, and parameter constraints, and labels values as source-reported or citation-reported when provenance can be determined. The records are available through a searchable web application. By connecting scientific literature to traceable, machine-readable data, LitCu- rate provides a reusable approach for transforming accumulated literature into resources for scientific analysis and computational modeling.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond the Vacuum: Combinatorial Strategy Selection for Competitor-Aware Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27631)
*Vaibhav Sourirajan,Yao Zhang,Himanshu Kumar,Sahil Wadhwa,Mann Patel,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种竞争感知的GEO策略选择方法，通过BOCS搜索最优改写策略组合，并利用偏好对和推理轨迹微调语言模型来预测最优改写策略，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEO方法孤立地选择改写策略，忽略了内容优化被广泛采用后策略选择的外部性——当所有人都在优化时，最优策略本身会发生变化，需要将竞争对手的策略纳入考虑。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：(1) 使用BOCS（组合结构贝叶斯优化）高效搜索改写策略空间；(2) 从BOCS的黑盒观测中生成偏好对和grounded推理轨迹，用于微调语言模型，使其能够分析文档语料库并提出最优改写策略组合。

Result: 在geo-bench和合成的竞争增强数据集geo-bench_comp上，多个印象指标均达到SOTA性能，优于现有的智能体方法和单一启发式方法；同时方法可迁移到多个分布外数据集，在不同领域、查询和文档类型上均表现有效。

Conclusion: 竞争感知的GEO策略选择是必要的且可行的，两阶段流水线方法兼顾了搜索效率与泛化能力，为GEO的后续研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) has emerged as a novel paradigm for transforming content to increase visibility in Large Language Model (LLM) responses. Traditional GEO methods, however, select rewriting strategies in isolation, ignoring a critical externality: as adoption of content optimization grows, optimal strategies for rewriting content change. We formalize GEO as a competitor-aware strategy selection problem and propose a two-phase pipeline to solve it: (1) We use Bayesian Optimization of Combinatorial Structures (BOCS) to efficiently search the space of rewriting strategies, (2) We generate preference pairs and grounded reasoning traces from the BOCS black-box observations to fine-tune a language model to analyze a document corpus and propose optimal rewriting strategy combinations. We achieve state-of-the-art performance across several impression metrics over existing agentic and single-heuristic methods on both geo-bench and our synthetically augmented competitive dataset geo-bench_comp. Our method also transfers to multiple out-of-distribution datasets, proving effective across domains, queries, and document types.

</details>


### [95] [A Versioned Unified Graph Index for Dynamic Timestamp-Aware Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27663)
*Jun Woo Chung,Weijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TiGER（时间集成图高效检索）方法，通过构建统一的版本化连接图结构，实现对动态向量数据集上任意时间范围的高效时间感知近似最近邻搜索，无需后过滤或合并，QPS最高提升5倍且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态向量数据（如实时推荐系统、日志分析）需要支持任意时间范围的时间感知近似最近邻搜索。现有方法通常需要通过后过滤或为每个时间片段建立子图来实现，效率低下且存储开销大。

Method: TiGER构建并维护一个统一图结构，利用"集成版本化连接"（integrated versioned connectivity）作为索引基础。所有向量共享同一个图，任意时间区间的查询可直接在统一图上进行，无需遍历无效向量，也无需后过滤、合并或为每个组合时间范围建立独立子图。

Result: 在经验评估中，TiGER相较于基于过滤或基于时间片段子图的基线方法，查询每秒（QPS）最多提升5倍，且不牺牲准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法能够支持实时推荐系统、日志分析等需要在动态时间分段数据上进行快速相似性搜索的场景，实现高效的时序分析。

Abstract: We present TiGER (Time-Integrated Graph for Efficient Retrieval), a novel approach for performing fast time-aware approximate nearest neighbor searches on dynamic vector datasets with flexibility over any possible time range. Our proposed algorithm builds and maintains a unified graph for all vectors by leveraging an index structure based on integrated versioned connectivity, allowing arbitrary time intervals to be queried directly on the unified graph without having to traverse invalid vectors. This forgoes the need for post-search filtering or merging, or separate graphs for each possible composite range. Empirical evaluations show that our method attains up to a 5x improvement in queries per second (QPS) without compromising accuracy over baselines based on filtering or per-time-segment sub-graphs. We believe that this method will enable efficient temporal analysis across evolving datasets in real-time recommendation systems, log analysis, and any scenario requiring fast similarity search over dynamic, time-segmented data.

</details>


### [96] [NormasTCU --- A Brazilian Portuguese IR Dataset and an Evaluation of LLM-as-a-Judge for Relevance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27746)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,Marcus Vinícius Borela de Castro,Leandro dos Santos Ribeiro,Leonardo Augusto da Silva Pacheco,Edans Flávius de Oliveira Sandes*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NormasTCU，一个巴西葡萄牙语法律领域的信息检索数据集，包含14,469份法律文档、46个查询和3,048个人工判断。通过评估三种LLM作为相关性评判者的表现，发现LLM存在正向评分偏差（MAE 0.46-0.66），与人类判断的一致性仅达到中等水平（Cohen's kappa 0.32-0.53），但在nDCG@10和MRR等排名感知指标下能产生高度相似的系统排名，建议LLM可支持大规模相关性评估但应避免用于精确率和召回率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄牙语信息检索领域缺乏公开数据集，而专业领域的相关性评估成本高昂。尽管LLM越来越多地用于相关性评估，但其在非英语专业领域的可靠性尚不清楚，因此需要构建数据集并系统评估LLM在此场景下的表现。

Method: 构建NormasTCU数据集（包含14,469份法律文档、46个查询和3,048个人工判断），使用三种LLM模型配合两种提示技术对812个查询-文档对进行相关性评分，并与15个IR系统的排名进行比较分析，评估LLM生成的qrels与人类参考qrels的一致性。

Result: LLM始终表现出正向评分偏差（平均绝对误差0.46-0.66，按0-2量表）；与人类判断的逐对一致性仅为一般到中等水平（Cohen's kappa 0.32-0.53）；在nDCG@10和MRR下系统排名高度相似（Kendall's tau≥0.90，但bootstrap置信区间并非始终高于该阈值）；P@10和R@10的可靠性较低；LLM排名有时与参考排名的相关性甚至强于单个人工标注。

Conclusion: LLM在nDCG或MRR等排名感知指标下可有效支持葡萄牙语专业语料的大规模相关性评估，但在依赖精确率或召回率时则应避免使用LLM进行评判。

Abstract: Portuguese Information Retrieval (IR) lacks public datasets, and relevance assessment for specialized collections remains costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support relevance assessment, their reliability in non-English specialized domains remains unclear. We introduce NormasTCU (https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeandroRibeiro/NormasTCU), a Brazilian Portuguese IR dataset with 14,469 legal documents, 46 queries, and 3,048 human judgments over 812 query-document pairs. Using NormasTCU, we evaluated LLM-as-a-judge for relevance assessment by prompting three models with two prompt techniques to grade these pairs. We then compared the rankings of 15 IR systems derived from LLM-generated and human reference qrels. LLMs consistently showed a positive scoring bias (mean absolute error: 0.46--0.66 on a 0-2 scale). Furthermore, pair-level agreement with human judgments achieved only fair to moderate levels, with Cohen's kappa ranging from 0.32 to 0.53. Despite this bias, LLM-generated judgments often yielded highly similar system rankings for nDCG@10 and MRR (observed Kendall's tau greater than or equal 0.90, although the bootstrap confidence intervals did not always remain above this threshold), but were less reliable for P@10 and R@10. Notably, LLM-based rankings were sometimes more strongly correlated with the reference ranking than individual human annotations were. As a practical implication, our results suggest that LLMs could effectively support scalable relevance assessment in specialized Portuguese corpora when evaluated using nDCG or MRR (rank-aware metrics), but they should be avoided when relying on precision or recall.

</details>


### [97] [LINE Conversation History Retrieval for Personal Memory RAG: Evaluating Search Representations and Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27809)
*Akito Hattori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对个人记忆检索增强生成（RAG）中的检索环节进行了案例研究，基于单个用户的36万条LINE聊天记录构建三种检索表示，对比多种检索器与混合配置，最终选定的组合在Recall@5上达到0.697，显著优于单一检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大规模语言模型（LLM）实现个人记忆检索增强生成（RAG）迈出第一步，探索从个人聊天记录中进行有效信息检索的方法。

Method: 将358,896条聊天消息分割为22,329个时间上连贯的块，构建三种检索表示（原始文本、生成摘要、embedding_text三者结合）；在100个评估问题上比较BM25、稠密向量检索和线性混合检索；探索了6种检索器配对和21种权重，共126种配置。

Result: 单个检索器中embedding_text_bm25表现最佳（Recall@5=0.584）；最佳混合配置（embedding_text_bm25 + embedding_text_vector，beta=0.45）达到Recall@5=0.697，MRR@5=0.595，nDCG@5=0.575，较单一检索器提升0.113且具有统计显著性；但与摘要混合方案差异不显著。聚合类问题表现较差，暗示平坦块级检索在处理跨时间、跨对话分布的证据时存在困难。

Conclusion: 混合检索在多模态表示上优于单一检索器，但当前为探索性的单用户、单标注者研究，且评估集与配置搜索集相同，未评估最终答案生成或对未见问题的泛化能力，结果需要谨慎解读。

Abstract: As an initial step toward personal memory retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for large language models (LLMs), this study presents a retrieval-only case study over one user's LINE conversation history. We segmented 358,896 messages into 22,329 temporally coherent chunks and constructed three search representations: raw_text, a generated summary, and embedding_text, which combines a summary with a raw-text excerpt and other fixed text. We compared BM25, dense vector retrieval, and linear hybrid retrieval on 100 evaluation questions verified by a single annotator. Among individual retrievers, embedding_text_bm25 achieved the highest point estimate, with Recall@5 of 0.584. We then explored six retriever pairings and 21 weights, for 126 configurations on the same evaluation set. The selected combination of embedding_text_bm25 and embedding_text_vector at beta = 0.45 achieved Recall@5 = 0.697, MRR@5 = 0.595, and nDCG@5 = 0.575. Its Recall@5 exceeded that of embedding_text_bm25 by 0.113, with a question-level paired percentile-bootstrap 95% confidence interval of [0.048, 0.184]. This interval is conditional on fixing the configuration selected on the same 100 questions and does not account for uncertainty from configuration selection or weight search. The difference from a summary-based hybrid at beta = 0.50 was 0.050, with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.013, 0.115], so no clear difference could be established. The 17 aggregate questions also yielded lower point estimates than the other question types, suggesting that flat chunk-level retrieval struggles when evidence is distributed across multiple times and conversations. This evaluation is an exploratory single-user, single-annotator study conducted on the same question set used for configuration search; it does not evaluate final answer generation or generalization to unseen questions.

</details>


### [98] [Personalized and Multi-View Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27826)
*Jaehyung Lim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Junyoung Kim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PMFRec，一种面向联邦推荐中冷物品场景的个性化多视图表示方法，通过个性化表示生成器和全局多视图编码器解决冷启动推荐中的个性化缺失、语义组合失败及训练通信低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐方法大多假设固定物品池，忽略了新物品持续到达的现实冷物品场景。在服务端无法访问客户端交互、客户端无法访问服务端私有物品属性特征的双边约束下，已有的联邦冷启动推荐方法存在三大结构性缺陷：缺乏个性化、将异构语义强制映射到单一嵌入空间导致组合失败、显式对齐分离的协同与属性表示导致训练和通信效率低下。

Method: PMFRec包含两个核心组件：(1) 个性化表示生成器，从属性特征生成用户特定的物品表示；(2) 全局多视图编码器，采用物品自适应门控和正交性目标捕获互补语义视图并减少跨视图冗余。同时，PMFRec将协同知识和属性知识融合到单一的交换物品表示中，消除了客户端侧显式正则化器的需求，降低了通信开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，PMFRec在冷物品推荐中持续优于强基线方法，并在用户级公平性、热场景适应性和本地差分隐私（LDP）下的鲁棒性方面均有进一步提升。

Conclusion: PMFRec通过个性化表示生成与多视图编码机制，有效解决了联邦推荐冷启动场景下的个性化缺失和效率瓶颈问题，为双边隐私约束下的冷物品推荐提供了可行且优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated recommendation (FedRec) enables personalized modeling without centralizing users' interaction histories, but most existing methods assume a fixed item pool and thus overlook the practical cold-item setting where new items continuously arrive. Under the dual-sided constraint, where the server cannot access clients' interactions while clients cannot access the server's proprietary item attribute features, prior federated cold-start recommendation approaches suffer from three structural limitations: a lack of personalization, compositionality failure caused by forcing heterogeneous semantics into a single embedding space, and training- and communication-inefficiency arising from explicit alignment between separate collaborative and attribute representations. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized and Multi-view Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation (PMFRec). PMFRec learns a personalized representation generator to produce user-specific item representations from attribute features, and introduces a global multi-view encoder with item-adaptive gating and an orthogonality objective to capture complementary semantic views while reducing cross-view redundancy. In addition, PMFRec fuses collaborative and attribute knowledge into a single exchanged item representation, eliminating the need for an explicit client-side regularizer and reducing communication overhead. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that PMFRec consistently outperforms strong baselines in cold-item recommendation and further improves user-level fairness, warm-scenario adaptability, and robustness under Local Differential Privacy (LDP).

</details>


### [99] [ITER: Interaction-Aware Retrieval for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27912)
*Haodong Chen,Shuai Wang,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出iter，一种具有代理交互感知的稠密检索器，利用代理轨迹学习信号进行训练，通过将主问题、当前子查询和先前子查询联合编码作为查询表示，并利用轨迹相对学习信号进行训练，在多个代理骨干网络上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器训练仅依赖当前步骤的子查询和搜索结果作为训练信号，未充分利用代理在先前交互中积累的信息，导致检索性能受限。

Method: iter将主问题、当前子查询和先前子查询融合为查询表示，并使用基于代理交互的轨迹相对学习信号进行训练。具体包括：利用主问题和先前子查询构建更稳健的查询表征，以及将之前访问过且有用的文档作为后续搜索中的冗余负样本，提供最强的轨迹相对监督信号。

Result: 在六个代理骨干网络（来自三个模型家族）上，iter相比现有代理轨迹训练的稠密检索器LRAT，在InfoSeek-Eval上平均提升7.5%，在BrowseComp-Plus上平均提升13.5%；同时相比依赖外部LLM评判信号和预搜索推理的AgentIR，展现出更强的跨代理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: iter验证了充分利用代理交互轨迹信息（尤其是主问题和先前子查询）能显著提升深度研究代理场景下的检索性能，代码已公开。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex user questions through an iterative sequence of search steps, where the agent autonomously formulates sub-queries to retrieve the evidence needed at each stage. However, existing retriever training typically relies only on the sub-query and its corresponding search results at the current step as training signals, leaving the information accumulated from previous interactions largely underutilized.
  We introduce iter, an agent interaction-aware dense retriever trained using agent trajectory learning signals. iter represents each query by incorporating not only the current sub-query, but also the main question and preceding sub-queries, and is trained using trajectory-relative learning signals derived from the agent's interactions.
  Across six agent backbones from three model families, iter consistently outperforms the existing agent-trajectory-trained dense retriever, LRAT, achieving an average improvement of 7.5% on InfoSeek-Eval and 13.5% on BrowseComp-Plus. iter also demonstrates stronger cross-agent robustness than AgentIR, a deep-research retriever that relies on external LLM-judge signals and the agent's pre-search reasoning. Ablations further show that the main question and previous sub-queries provide the most robust query representation, while previously visited and useful documents, used as redundancy negatives in subsequent searches, provide the strongest trajectory-relative supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/ielab/ITER.

</details>


### [100] [Information-Guided Selective Modality-Interest Alignment for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27950)
*Wenze Ma,Chenyu Sun,Yanmin Zhu,Qiwen Gu,Xuhao Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AMUR框架，通过信息论视角指导的模态-兴趣选择性对齐，优化多模态推荐中的模态利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMRec方法在模态对齐过程中往往是隐式或启发式的，缺乏清晰的目标来选择与用户兴趣更匹配的模态信号，导致无关模态信号可能引入噪声。

Method: AMUR首先对模态图结构进行面向用户行为的精炼，然后选择性对齐跨模态间共享的兴趣相关语义，同时保留模态特有的互补信息。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，AMUR优于多个竞争性基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过信息引导的模态-兴趣选择性对齐，AMUR能够有效增强与用户兴趣相关的模态信息，同时减弱不对齐信号的影响，从而提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation (MMRec) aims to enhance recommendation performance by leveraging rich item content from multiple modalities. However, directly incorporating all modality information does not necessarily lead to better preference modeling, since user interests are often more related to a subset of modality signals, while other signals may be weakly aligned with user preferences or even introduce noise. Although recent MMRec methods improve modality utilization through invariant learning, attention mechanisms, graph refinement, or contrastive learning, their alignment processes are often implicit or heuristic and lack a clear objective for selecting modality signals that better match user interests.
  In this paper, we propose AMUR, an information-guided selective modality-interest alignment framework for multimodal recommendation. Inspired by an information-theoretic view, AMUR aims to enhance modality information that is more related to user interests while reducing the influence of less aligned signals. Specifically, AMUR first refines modality graph structures towards user behavior, and then selectively aligns shared interest-related semantics across modalities. This enables AMUR to improve modality-interest alignment while preserving useful modality-specific complementary information. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AMUR over competitive baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Wenze1/AMUR.

</details>


### [101] [HubMixer: Progressive Latent Hub Mixing for Parameter-Efficient Feature Interaction in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27991)
*Jie Zhou,Zixian Gong,Wenhao Li,Chang Liu,Enzhao Shen,Bo Liu,Xu Guo,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HubMixer，一种参数高效的潜在中心混合架构，用于推荐系统中的特征交互。通过"归纳-交互-读出"范式，利用少量可学习的潜在中心组织异构特征token的交互，替代直接在原始异构token空间中混合的思路。离线实验超越SOTA，在线A/B测试在快手短剧招聘业务中简历提交转化率提升5.48%，已全面部署生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐和广告系统中的令牌混合架构在硬件效率和大规模部署上表现优异，但推荐token本质上是异构的（用户画像、物品属性、行为序列、上下文特征等），直接在所有原始异构token空间中进行混合是参数低效的，因为模型需要隐式发现哪些特征组应交互以及如何路由交互，缺乏显式结构引导。

Method: HubMixer引入少量可学习的潜在中心来组织特征交互，采用三阶段范式：(1) Hub归纳：通过交叉注意力让潜在中心查询输入token，将异构token压缩到紧凑的潜在中心空间；(2) Hub交互：在更干净的潜在中心空间中执行高阶特征交互；(3) token条件读出：每个原始token选择性读取交互后的中心信息，注入全局交互语义的同时保留token级字段身份。

Result: 在工业推荐任务的离线实验中，HubMixer优于现有SOTA模型。在快手短剧招聘业务的在线A/B测试中，简历提交转化率实现了统计学显著的5.48%提升，且HubMixer已完全部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: HubMixer通过潜在中心机制解决了异构推荐token直接混合带来的参数低效问题，以"归纳-交互-读出"范式实现了高效且可部署的特征交互建模，实验结果和工业部署均验证了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Learning effective feature interactions is central to industrial recommendation and advertising ranking systems. Recent token-mixing architectures simplify self-attention with lightweight mixing operators, improving hardware efficiency and enabling large-scale deployment. However, recommendation tokens are fundamentally heterogeneous: user profiles, item attributes, behavioral sequences, context features, statistical signals, and business-side features live in different semantic spaces and interact in sparse, sample-specific patterns. Directly mixing all tokens in the raw heterogeneous token space may therefore be parameter-inefficient, as the model must implicitly discover which feature groups should interact and how such interactions should be routed. In the paper, we propose HubMixer, a parameter-efficient latent hub mixing architecture for feature interaction in recommendation. Instead of directly mixing raw feature tokens, HubMixer introduces a small set of learnable latent hubs to organize feature interactions through an `induction--interaction--readout` paradigm. First, hub induction summarizes heterogeneous tokens into compact latent hubs, where latent hubs query input tokens through cross-attention. Second, hub interaction performs high-order interaction in the cleaner latent hub space. Third, token-conditioned readout lets each original token selectively read from the interacted hubs, injecting global interaction semantics while preserving token-level field identity. Extensive offline experiments on industrial recommendation tasks show that HubMixer outperforms the SOTA models. Online A/B testing in the Kuaishou short-video recruitment business further shows a statistically significant 5.48% improvement in resume submission conversion rate, and HubMixer has been fully deployed in production.

</details>


### [102] [Every Article Deserves a Video: Contextual Video Matching for Digital Publishers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28359)
*Arnaud Corone,Brice Pierre de la Briere,Gladys Roch,Samuel Leonardo Gracio,Yassine Bouher,Parvati Chauchaix*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dailymotion开发的"Contextual Video Matching"系统，利用大语言模型和文本嵌入技术，自动将相关视频匹配到基于文本的网页和文章中，解决了大规模出版商手动选择视频的低效问题。系统已在Dailymotion生产环境部署，被数百家出版商采用，显著提升了用户参与度和体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字出版商面临海量内容目录管理挑战，在文本文章中有效嵌入相关视频对变现和用户留存至关重要。然而，面对出版商自身的视频库甚至全球Dailymotion目录，手动选择视频在大规模场景下不可行，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 该系统通过结合大语言模型（LLMs）和文本嵌入技术，实现视频与文本网页/文章之间的自动匹配。系统架构包括视频内容分析与文本语义理解的融合，提供可扩展的匹配方案，并已在Dailymotion生产环境中进行了详细的设计、评估和部署。

Result: 系统自上线以来已被数百家出版商采用，显著提高了用户参与度，通过提供高度相关的视频内容丰富了用户体验，验证了该方案在大规模生产环境中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM和文本嵌入的自动化视频匹配方案是解决大规模内容匹配问题的有效途径，能够在不牺牲相关性的前提下实现可扩展部署，为数字出版行业提供了实用的技术方案。

Abstract: As digital publishers face the challenge of managing massive content catalogs, the ability to effectively embed relevant video within text-based articles has become critical for both monetization and user retention. However, manual selection is impractical for large scale publishers, especially when navigating their own extensive video libraries or the entire global Dailymotion catalog. In this paper, we present the "Contextual Video Matching" system, a solution that automatically matches relevant videos with text-heavy web pages and articles. By leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and textual embeddings, we provide a scalable solution for publishers to efficiently combine video content with their articles. We discuss in detail the motivations, architecture, evaluations, and deployment of this system within Dailymotion's production environment. Since its launch, the system has been adopted by hundreds of publishers, significantly increasing user engagement and enriching user experiences with highly relevant video content.

</details>


### [103] [SG-UMP: Sequence-Guided Universal Multimodal Prioritization Calculation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28503)
*Xinyi Zhang,Yutong Li,Peijie Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-UMP，一个即插即用的插件框架，通过模块组合器（Module Combiner）和模块路由器（Module Router）动态调整多模态信息处理顺序，解决现有多模态序列推荐方法难以适应用户偏好异质性和数据集模态偏差的问题，在四个真实数据集上显著提升了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSR方法未能捕捉用户级别的偏好异质性以及数据集级别的模态偏差，导致其在不同用户和数据集上的适应性有限。

Method: 提出SG-UMP框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）Module Combiner，用于灵活组合多模态处理模块；（2）Module Router，用于动态决定模块的处理顺序，从而同时适配不同用户的偏好和不同数据集的特征。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SG-UMP在不同骨干模型和多模态设置下均能一致地提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: SG-UMP作为一种通用即插即用插件，能够有效增强MSR中的多模态信息处理能力，具有较好的跨用户和跨数据集的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommendation (MSR) improves recommendation by incorporating heterogeneous information such as text, images, and user interactions. However, existing MSR methods often fail to capture user-level preference heterogeneity and dataset-level modality bias, limiting their adaptability across users and datasets. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{S}equence-\textbf{G}uided \textbf{U}niversal \textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{P}rioritization Calculation Framework (\textbf{SG-UMP}), a plug-and-play plugin for enhancing multimodal information processing in MSR. SG-UMP includes a Module Combiner for flexible multimodal processing and a Module Router for dynamic module ordering, enabling adaptation to both user preferences and dataset characteristics. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that SG-UMP consistently improves recommendation performance across different backbones and multimodal settings. The code is available at https://github.com/esemsc-xz524/SG-UMP .

</details>


### [104] [QUEST: A Query and Extraction System for Topics in Asylum Law Application Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28555)
*Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了QUEST系统，用于从丹麦庇护申请上诉文件中提取与可信度评估相关的因素，将该问题建模为信息检索任务，并结合合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估来识别可信度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 庇护申请的法律判决文档长且复杂异构，当申请被拒绝时，判断原始申请中的信息可信度是否是决定拒绝的关键因素，是上诉处理中的核心问题。

Method: 将问题建模为信息检索任务，采用合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估相结合的方法，并在传统检索评估指标之外，提出了基于可信度因素的新型领域特定评估方法。

Result: 实验表明，使用基于可信度的相关性评估来估算性能时面临更大的挑战，反映出该任务本身的难度。

Conclusion: QUEST系统能够从庇护上诉材料中提取可信度相关因素，但该任务仍具有较高难度，基于可信度的评估指标揭示了自动方法在不同类型可信度指标上的表现差异。

Abstract: Legal decisions on asylum applications consist of long, complex, and heterogeneous documents, covering narrative applicant interviews, original decisions, and additional supporting materials. If an application is rejected, a critical question in processing an appeal is whether the credibility of the information in the original application was a factor that determined the original decision. In this paper, we present the QUEST system (Query and Extraction System for Topics) to extract and identify factors relating to credibility assessments in two datasets of Danish asylum application appeals. QUEST frames this problem as an information retrieval task, combining synthetic query generation, topic extraction, and relevance assessment to identify information related to credibility indicators in appeals board application materials. In addition to standard retrieval evaluation metrics, we propose a new type of domain-specific assessments distinct from the traditional relevance to evaluate the performance of the tested systems with respect to credibility factors. In this way, we obtain insights about how well automatic methods can return answers for different types of indicators appearing in asylum appeals. Our results indicate that there is an increased challenge when estimating performance using credibility-based relevance assessments, thus pointing to the difficulty of the task.

</details>


### [105] [PULSAR: Pooled Unified Late-Interaction Search and Retrieval for Enterprise Visual Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28572)
*Benjamin Constable,Anup Roy,Vishal Sharma,Rishabh Upadhyay,Robin Mills,Aidan Millar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PULSAR是Mubadala Investment Company部署的视觉优先检索系统，通过分池两阶段晚期交互索引，在ViDoRe V3上将向量搜索延迟降低15.1倍，生产环境延迟156ms，QPS提升约88倍，单页成本比OCR+verbalisation基线降低约20倍，自2026年3月起已服务78,000份文档和约240万页。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资者需要频繁检索高频更新的视觉密集型文件（如pitch decks、董事会资料包），但传统的OCR+图表述化流程在大规模下成本高昂且容易丢失图表细节。

Method: 采用冻结的ColPali风格骨干网络对页面图像进行索引，使用分池的两阶段晚期交互索引：紧凑页面摘要支持初步检索，随后在更细粒度的池化表示上执行精确MaxSim重排序。

Result: ViDoRe V3上中位向量搜索延迟降低15.1倍，NDCG@10和Recall@10损失小于0.01绝对值；生产环境延迟156ms；并发负载下QPS约为未池化索引的88倍；事件驱动摄取路径每页成本约为基线的1/20；生产环境top K下answer-fact召回率是OCR+verbalisation基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: PULSAR证明了视觉优先检索架构在生产环境中的可行性和高效性，不仅大幅降低延迟与成本，还显著提升检索质量，已在Mubadala实际业务中大规模部署并产生可观收益。

Abstract: Institutional investors search visually dense pitch decks, board packs, and diligence materials that change hourly near deal closing. OCR followed by figure verbalisation is costly to refresh at this scale and can lose chart detail. We present PULSAR, a production vision-first retrieval system deployed at Mubadala Investment Company. PULSAR indexes page images with a frozen ColPali-style backbone and uses a pooled two-stage late-interaction index: compact page summaries support initial retrieval, followed by exact MaxSim rescoring over a finer pooled representation. On ViDoRe V3, this design reduces median vector-search latency by 15.1 times against an unpooled configuration with less than 0.01 absolute NDCG@10 and Recall@10 loss; production median vector-search latency is 156 ms. Under concurrent load, the pooled index sustains approximately 88 times higher QPS than an unpooled index. The event-driven ingestion path is estimated to be approximately 20 times cheaper per page than the OCR+verbalisation baseline it replaced. Since March 2026, PULSAR has served 78 thousand documents and approximately 2.4 million pages across more than 3,000 deals. At the production top K, it more than doubles answer-fact recall over the OCR+verbalisation baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Characterizing the I/O Behavior of HPC Applications through Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27642)
*Njoud O. Almaaitah,David E. Singh,Taylan Özden,Jesus Carretero,Raffaele Montella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个框架，利用ElastiSim批量系统模拟器，基于从HPCIO分析库获取的真实应用I/O轨迹，重现并行应用的I/O访问模式，并通过案例研究展示其对I/O性能特征和拥塞分析的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行应用产生大量I/O操作，具有多种访问模式、数据暂存和竞争风险等复杂性，因此需要优化HPC系统的I/O性能，关键是要理解应用的I/O行为。

Method: 基于ElastiSim模拟器构建框架，利用HPCIO分析仓库中真实应用的I/O访问轨迹生成模拟应用，在模拟环境中重现真实的I/O访问模式。

Result: 通过多个真实世界应用轨迹的详细案例研究，展示了该框架能够提供对I/O应用性能特征的深入洞察，包括基于I/O访问模式的拥塞分析。

Conclusion: 该建模框架能有效帮助理解和优化HPC应用中并行I/O的性能特征，为I/O拥塞分析和性能优化提供支持。

Abstract: Parallel applications process large amounts of data, leading to intensive parallel I/O operations. These operations can exhibit different levels of complexity, including, among others, multiple I/O access patterns, data staging, and contention risks. Therefore, in order to exploit high-performance computing (HPC) systems efficiently and optimize the I/O performance, it is crucial to consider the I/O behaviour of the HPC applications. In this work, we have developed a framework that reproduces the I/O access pattern of real applications in a simulated environment provided by ElastiSim, a batch-system simulator for rigid, malleable, and evolving workloads. The simulated applications are generated based on I/O traces captured from real applications provided by the HPC Input/Output (HPCIO) analysis repository. The HPCIO analysis database includes traces combined with information about real applications' performance across different parallel I/O libraries and layers of the I/O stack. We have conducted detailed case studies of real-world applications' traces to demonstrate how the proposed modeling framework can provide insights into the performance characteristics of I/O applications, including the I/O congestion analysis based on the application's I/O access pattern.

</details>


### [107] [TerraceMoE: A Cost Model for Hierarchical MoE All-to-All Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27874)
*Weicheng Xue,Bingqiang Wang,Li Yuan,Huihui Zhou,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在通信调用级别评估MoE专家并行训练中层级两跳调度的成本模型，并通过验证门自动撤回不满足条件的代码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级两跳调度可减少MoE训练中的慢速网络流量，但引入了第二次集合通信和到达侧算子链，需要量化这一权衡。

Method: 构建通信调用级别的成本模型，采用验证门（validation gates）机制，当失败时主动从代码中撤回相应能力而非报告警告。

Result: 在参考几何配置下，修正后的有效盈亏平衡层级比为3.98（实测PyTorch到达链）、1.49（假设融合目标）、1.10（零实现开销）。四个通信级别语料库通过门检查，但漂移探测和步骤级门失败。

Conclusion: 比率敏感性分析而非部署预测；测得平台均未进入层级调度区间。验证损失代价小但非零（+0.0034 nats），下游等价性结果因估算来源不完整而无法独立复现。代码、校准常数和验证门已开源。

Abstract: Hierarchical two-hop dispatch can reduce slow-fabric traffic in expert-parallel Mixture-of-Experts training, but it adds a second collective and an arrival-side operator chain. We present a cost model for screening that trade at the communication-call level, bounded by validation gates that withdraw a capability in code when they fail rather than reporting a caveat. At a reference geometry with 16 groups of 8 ranks, $q=3$, $H=2048$, and 4096 tokens per rank, the corrected effective breakeven hierarchy ratio is 3.98 for the measured PyTorch arrival chain, 1.49 for a hypothetical fused target, and 1.10 at zero implementation overhead. These are ratio-only sensitivity results, not deployment predictions: platform A measures 1.03, platform B has no separated fast/slow measurement, and neither machine measured here reaches the hierarchical regime. Four communication-level corpora pass their gates; a drift probe and the step-level gate fail. The latter failure is enforced in code, so we make no training-throughput prediction. The enabling routing constraint fixes per-token fan-out and per-selected-group quota, while aggregate per-peer counts remain data-dependent. Its measured validation-loss cost is small but nonzero (+0.0034 nats); downstream equivalence is reported with incomplete estimator provenance and is therefore not independently reconstructible from the artifact. Code, calibration constants and the validation gates are at https://github.com/weich97/TerraceMoE-simulator.

</details>


### [108] [Learning-Augmented Heuristics: Simple, yet Smart, Robust and Interpretable Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27975)
*Haocheng Xia,William Nixon,Bintang Dwi Marthen,Pranav Bhandari,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Learning-Augmented Heuristics (LAH)框架，通过解耦数据面和控制面，学习静态缓存启发式算法的缓存级参数。基于此实现了S4-FIFO算法，在1035条评估轨迹上相比S3-FIFO平均效率提升26%，相比最先进的3L-Cache提升8%，且鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能缓存算法（如ARC、LRB）虽能自适应工作负载，但存在目标失配和不稳定性问题；而静态启发式算法（如2Q、S3-FIFO）简单高效但缺乏自适应性。作者希望结合两者的优势，既保留静态启发式的简单高速特性，又获得智能算法的自适应能力。

Method: 提出LAH框架：1) 解耦数据面和控制面，数据面保持简单高速的读写操作，控制面进行异步学习；2) 使用缓存级特征学习静态启发式算法的最优参数；3) 在4140条生产轨迹上预训练单一模型，嵌入到S4-FIFO中学习最优缓存参数；4) 利用语言模型为配置选择提供可解释的理由。

Result: 在1035条评估轨迹上，S4-FIFO相比S3-FIFO平均效率提升26%，相比最先进的3L-Cache提升8%。在最差轨迹上，S4-FIFO相比FIFO的未命中率仅增加0.8%，而3L-Cache增加了8.8%，表明其鲁棒性更强。此外，决策可被语言模型解释。

Conclusion: LAH框架成功地将学习能力融入静态启发式缓存算法，在不牺牲数据面速度的前提下获得了更高的效率和鲁棒性，且决策具有可解释性，为缓存管理提供了一条实用的学习增强路径。

Abstract: Caching is widely used across the system stack to improve performance and efficiency, with eviction algorithms at its core. Existing cache eviction policies fall into two broad categories: static heuristics (e.g., 2Q, S3-FIFO) and smart algorithms (e.g., ARC, LRB). Smart caches can adapt to workloads and have the potential to achieve higher efficiency and robustness than static heuristics. However, we find that existing smart caches suffer from objective mismatches and instability. We introduce Learning-Augmented Heuristics (LAH), a framework that learns the cache-level parameters of static heuristics. By decoupling the data and control planes, LAH supports simple, high-speed data reads and writes on the data plane, while performing occasional asynchronous learning on the control plane using cache-level features. We demonstrate the effectiveness of LAH through S4-FIFO, a Smart S3-FIFO cache eviction algorithm. We pre-train a single model on 4,140 production traces and embed it in S4-FIFO to learn optimal cache parameters. On 1,035 evaluation traces, S4-FIFO improves the mean efficiency by 26% compared to S3-FIFO and by 8% compared to 3L-Cache, the best state-of-the-art algorithm. S4-FIFO is also robust---increasing miss ratio over FIFO by 0.8% on the worst trace, whereas 3L-Cache increases FIFO's miss ratio by 8.8%. Finally, S4-FIFO's decisions are also interpretable: a language model can provide a rationale for why a particular configuration was chosen.

</details>


### [109] [Great Expectations: Benchmarking the Real-World Performance of RVV 1.0 in HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28097)
*Stepan Nassyr,Prateek Chawla,Daniel Seibel,Jayesh Badwaik,Kaveh Haghighi Mood,Andreas Herten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在RVV 1.0硬件（SiFive X280、SpacemiT X60/X100/A100、T-Head C920v2）上通过HPC标准基准测试（BLAS、FFTW、HPL、HPCG等）评估RISC-V在高性能计算领域的可行性，并与NVIDIA Grace进行对比，发现RVV 1.0相对标量执行有显著性能提升，但仍存在硬件实现相关的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RISC-V向量扩展（RVV 1.0）已获批准并被新商用芯片采用，但其在高性能计算（HPC）领域的实际表现和成熟度缺乏系统性的评估。该研究旨在重新审视RISC-V是否足以胜任HPC工作负载。

Method: 选取最新的RVV 1.0商用硬件（SiFive X280、SpacemiT X60和X100/A100、T-Head C920v2），使用标准HPC基准测试（BLAS、FFTW、HPL、HPCG）及合成工作负载（STREAM、FMA吞吐量）进行系统性评测，并以NVIDIA Grace作为ARM64性能对标参考。

Result: RVV 1.0相比标量执行带来了显著的性能提升，但不同硬件在具体实现上仍存在差异和挑战，与NVIDIA Grace等成熟HPC芯片相比仍有差距。

Conclusion: RISC-V（包括RVV）要在HPC领域成为主流仍需克服硬件实现层面的障碍，本文详细阐述了这些性能特征和剩余挑战。

Abstract: Following the ratification of the RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV 1.0), new commercially available silicon has been adopting the extension. This paper revisits the question of RISC-V viability for High-Performance-Computing (HPC) by benchmarking the latest RVV 1.0-capable hardware (SiFive X280 (Tenstorrent Blackhole), SpacemiT X60 (K1) and X100/A100 (K3), and T-Head C920v2 (Sophon SG2044)). We assess these platforms using standard HPC benchmarks (BLAS, FFTW, HPL, HPCG) and synthetic workloads (STREAM, FMA throughput) and compare them to a state-of-the-art HPC ARM64 chip (NVIDIA Grace). Our findings show that while RVV 1.0 delivers significant performance improvements over scalar execution, hardware-specific implementation challenges remain. We detail these performance characteristics and discuss the remaining hurdles for RISC-V, including RVV, to become a mainstay in the HPC landscape.

</details>


### [110] [Memory-efficient GPU pipelines for real-time non-line-of-sight reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28183)
*Alfonso López-Ruiz,Diego Royo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对非视距（NLOS）成像中重建阶段成为性能瓶颈的问题，重构了两种基于波的算法（f-k迁移和phasor-fields）的GPU实现，通过离线构建环形核、融合内核、warp级光子分箱、批量变换、CUDA图重放和FP16存储等优化手段，实现了比参考流水线快42倍、比最快公开GPU基线快14倍的性能提升，同时内存占用降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLOS成像依赖SPAD阵列采集大量光子时间戳数据进行逆问题求解，随着SPAD阵列吞吐量提升，重建计算成为限制整个系统性能的核心瓶颈，现有GPU实现效率不足以支持流式处理和大规模高分辨率重建。

Method: 该方法对两种波基算法（f-k迁移和phasor-fields）进行全面的GPU重构：在phasor-fields中利用环的解析傅里叶变换预先离线组装环形核，避免运行时生成稠密传播核；同时采用融合内核、warp级光子分箱、批量变换、CUDA图重放以及仅在瓶颈处应用的FP16存储等多项优化手段，并设计了消融实验验证各方案的有效性。

Result: 相比参考流式流水线实现最高提速42倍，相比最快的公开GPU基线最高提速14倍，内存占用降至最低2.5%，在相同硬件上支持更大更精细的重建，或在极低内存预算下完成相当规模的重建。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的GPU性能优化，重建阶段不再成为NLOS成像的瓶颈，所释放的帧预算为下一代NLOS视频处理提供了三种去噪策略的可行空间。

Abstract: Non-line-of-sight (NLOS) imaging reconstructs scenes hidden around a corner from indirect light recorded by a single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD). A single reconstruction is a large inverse problem: billions of photon timestamps must be binned, moved through memory, transformed and inverted. As SPAD arrays raise acquisition throughput, reconstruction becomes the limiting stage. We rebuild the GPU execution of two established wave-based algorithms, f-k migration and phasor-fields, for both streaming and offline processing. On the phasor-fields side we assemble the ring-and-radius kernels of previous work once and offline, using the analytic Fourier transform of a ring, so the propagation kernel never exists in dense form at runtime, reducing the memory and bandwidth. We reorganize the pipeline of both algorithms with fused kernels, warp-level photon binning, batched transforms, CUDA graph replay, and FP16 storage applied only where it reduces the actual bottleneck. Our implementations are up to 42x faster than the reference streaming pipeline and up to 14x faster than the fastest published GPU baseline, all while using a fraction of the memory (down to 2.5%), enabling vastly larger and finer reconstructions on the same hardware, or comparable ones within a much lower memory budget. We report an ablation of each implementation choice and propose three denoising strategies enabled by the resulting frame budget for next-generation NLOS video processing.

</details>
